这两个处理器演示了 create 和 update。GET、LIST、DELETE 和 PATCH 仍然需要处理器：GET 把条目的修订版本投射到对象 RV；LIST 把每个条目的 RV 以及 Range 快照 RV 投射到列表元数据；这两个读取都不把对象写回。DELETE 可以使用一个 RV 前置条件并返回先前的对象。删除事件单独携带删除提交的修订版本。对于 PATCH，使用带显式 RV 的对象形式 JSON Merge Patch：递归穿过对象，替换数组，删除被设为 null 的字段，然后校验身份并以 CAS 提交。API 契约 提供了路由、类型、错误和示例。

[22]

The remaining pieces of mini-apiserver are:

mini-apiserver 剩下的部分是：

[23]
  • Validation rules — type-specific (validatePod, validateService, etc.), ~30 lines each.
  • Defaulters — assign restartPolicy=Always, terminationGracePeriodSeconds=30, ClusterIP allocation.
  • The path parser (/api/v1/... vs /apis/<group>/<version>/...).
  • Discovery — /api, /apis, /api/v1 endpoints listing what's available (so kubectl-mini can do introspection).
  • The watch handler (next section).
  • 校验规则——特定于类型（validatePod、validateService 等），每个约 30 行。
  • 默认值设置器——分配 restartPolicy=Always、terminationGracePeriodSeconds=30、ClusterIP 分配。
  • 路径解析器（/api/v1/... 对 /apis/<group>/<version>/...）。
  • 发现——/api、/apis、/api/v1 端点列出可用的内容（以便 kubectl-mini 能做内省）。
  • watch 处理器（下一节）。

The object interface needs metadata access and a deep-copy operation. Keep /status and /binding in the path parser: status writes replace only status after an RV check; unchanged status returns without a new write. Binding uses POST, checks the Pod identity/version and target Node, then CAS-updates nodeName; repeating the same assignment is idempotent, a different assignment conflicts. These narrow writes preserve fields owned by other components. API Server owns the embedded KV; all other processes use HTTP.

对象接口需要元数据访问和一个深拷贝操作。在路径解析器中保留 /status 和 /binding：状态写入在一次 RV 检查之后只替换状态；未改变的状态直接返回，不产生新的写入。Binding 使用 POST，检查 Pod 的身份/版本和目标 Node，然后 CAS 更新 nodeName；重复相同的分配是幂等的，不同的分配则会冲突。这些窄写入保留其他组件拥有的字段。API Server 拥有嵌入的 KV；所有其他进程使用 HTTP。

[24]

9.3 The resourceVersion Contract

9.3 resourceVersion 契约

[25]

This is where most apiserver subtlety lives. The rules mini-apiserver enforces:

apiserver 的大部分微妙之处就在这里。mini-apiserver 强制执行的规则：

  1. Listing returns a resourceVersion — the highest revision at the moment of the list snapshot. This is the metadata.resourceVersion on the list, not on individual items. (Per-item RVs are different and reflect when that item last changed.)
  2. A watch with resourceVersion=X delivers events starting strictly after revision X. The list-then-watch sequence (list at RV=L, then watch from L) is gap-free because watch's start-exclusive matches list's snapshot-at-L.
  3. A watch with no RV initializes from a current snapshot: send its objects as ADDED, then a BOOKMARK at the snapshot RV, then newer events. Retry the snapshot if compaction overtakes the Range-to-Watch interval.
  4. A watch with resourceVersion=0 means "start from any point you have; I just want to receive any events you've got" — useful for informers that don't care about catchup.
  5. A compacted RV returns 410 Gone. Clients must re-list and re-watch.
  1. 列表返回一个 resourceVersion——列表快照那一刻的最高修订版本。这是列表上的 metadata.resourceVersion，不是单个条目上的。（每个条目的 RV 是不同的，反映该条目最后一次更改的时间。）
  2. 带 resourceVersion=X 的 watch 从严格晚于修订版本 X 之后开始投递事件。先列表后 watch 的序列（在 RV=L 列表，然后从 L 开始 watch）是无间隙的，因为 watch 的起始排他匹配列表的 L 处快照。
  3. 没有 RV 的 watch 从当前快照初始化：把其对象作为 ADDED 发送，然后在快照 RV 处发送一个 BOOKMARK，再发送较新的事件。如果压缩越过了 Range 到 Watch 的间隔，就重试快照。
  4. resourceVersion=0 的 watch 意味着“从你拥有的任意点开始；我只想接收你已有的任何事件”——对不关心追赶的 informer 有用。
  5. 一个已压缩的 RV 返回 410 Gone。客户端必须重新列表并重新 watch。
[26]

Chapter 05 (apiserver) §watch-semantics walks every edge case. Chapter 08 (controller pattern) §relist-on-error explains how informers handle the 410 Gone.

第 05 章（apiserver）§watch-semantics 走遍每一个边界情况。第 08 章（控制器模式）§relist-on-error 解释了 informer 如何处理 410 Gone。

9.4 Optimistic Concurrency

9.4 乐观并发

Every write path goes through kv.Put(key, body, ifMatchRV). If two clients PUT the same object concurrently, one wins and the other gets HTTP 409 Conflict. The client is expected to (a) re-GET to see the current state, (b) re-apply its intent, (c) retry the PUT with the new RV.

每个写入路径都经过 kv.Put(key, body, ifMatchRV)。如果两个客户端并发地 PUT 同一个对象，一个胜出，另一个得到 HTTP 409 Conflict。客户端被期望 (a) 重新 GET 以查看当前状态，(b) 重新应用其意图，(c) 用新的 RV 重试 PUT。

This is the concurrency model of Kubernetes. Controllers don't lock objects; they observe, decide, write with CAS, and retry on conflict. The only thing that needs distributed locking (leader election among multiple controller-manager replicas) uses a Lease object with the same CAS mechanism.

这是 Kubernetes 的那个并发模型。控制器不锁定对象；它们观察、决定、以 CAS 写入，并在冲突时重试。唯一需要分布式锁的东西（多个 controller-manager 副本之间的领导者选举）使用一个带有相同 CAS 机制的 Lease 对象。

10. Phase 5 — The Watch Endpoint

10. 阶段 5 — Watch 端点

The watch endpoint is what makes Kubernetes eventual but bounded: every interested client subscribes once, then receives a continuous stream of events. Without watch, every controller would poll, the apiserver would melt, and the cluster would scale to ~5 nodes.

watch 端点使 Kubernetes 最终但有界：每个感兴趣的客户端订阅一次，然后接收持续的事件流。没有 watch，每个控制器都会轮询，apiserver 会熔化，而集群会扩展到约 5 个节点。

10.1 The Wire Format

10.1 传输格式

Kubernetes Watch sends a stream of JSON events. HTTP/1.1 may use chunked transfer encoding; HTTP/2 uses DATA frames. Read complete JSON events rather than relying on transport chunk boundaries. The body is:

Kubernetes Watch 发送一个 JSON 事件流。HTTP/1.1 可以使用分块传输编码；HTTP/2 使用 DATA 帧。读取完整的 JSON 事件，而不是依赖传输的分块边界。主体是：

[27]
{"type":"ADDED","object":{...full object...}}
{"type":"MODIFIED","object":{...}}
{"type":"DELETED","object":{...}}
{"type":"BOOKMARK","object":{"metadata":{"resourceVersion":"12345"}}}
{"type":"ERROR","object":{"status":"Failure","code":410,"reason":"Gone"}}

{"type":"ADDED","object":{...完整对象...}}
{"type":"MODIFIED","object":{...}}
{"type":"DELETED","object":{...}}
{"type":"BOOKMARK","object":{"metadata":{"resourceVersion":"12345"}}}
{"type":"ERROR","object":{"status":"Failure","code":410,"reason":"Gone"}}

We will use the same format, just over HTTP/1.1 chunked transfer encoding (HTTP/2 is a deployment detail).

我们将使用相同的格式，只是通过 HTTP/1.1 分块传输编码（HTTP/2 是部署细节）。

10.2 The Handler

10.2 处理器

func (s *APIServer) handleWatch(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, info *ResourceInfo, ns string) {
    prefix := storagePrefix(info, ns)
    query := r.URL.Query()
    initial := !query.Has("resourceVersion")
    startRev := int64(0)
    if !initial {
        var err error
        startRev, err = strconv.ParseInt(query.Get("resourceVersion"), 10, 64)
        if err != nil || startRev < 0 { http.Error(w, "invalid RV", 422); return }
    }
    flusher, ok := w.(http.Flusher)
    if !ok { http.Error(w, "no streaming", 500); return }
    var snapshot []kv.Pair
    var ch <-chan kv.Event
    var errs <-chan error
    var cancel func()
    for {
        if r.Context().Err() != nil { return }
        if initial { snapshot, startRev = s.kv.Range(prefix) }
        var err error
        ch, errs, cancel, err = s.kv.Watch(prefix, startRev)
        if initial && err == kv.ErrCompacted { continue }
        if err == kv.ErrCompacted { http.Error(w, "expired RV", 410); return }
        if err == kv.ErrSlowConsumer { http.Error(w, "slow consumer", 503); return }
        if err != nil { http.Error(w, err.Error(), 500); return }
        break
    }
    defer cancel()
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    w.WriteHeader(200)
    flusher.Flush()
    // emitKVObject decodes bytes, projects the supplied RV, writes JSON + newline,
    // and returns any decode/write error. The other emit helpers also return errors.
    if initial {
        for _, item := range snapshot {
            if err := emitKVObject(w, info, "ADDED", item.Value, item.ModRevision); err != nil { return }
            flusher.Flush()
        }
        if err := emitBookmark(w, startRev); err != nil { return }
        flusher.Flush()
    }
    delivered := startRev
    ticker := time.NewTicker(30 * time.Second)
    defer ticker.Stop()
    for {
        select {
        case <-r.Context().Done(): return
        case <-ticker.C:
            if err := emitBookmark(w, delivered); err != nil { return }
            flusher.Flush()
        case ev, ok := <-ch:
            if !ok {
                if err, failed := <-errs; failed {
                    if err == kv.ErrCompacted { emitErr(w, 410, "Expired", "history compacted")
                    } else { emitErr(w, 503, "ServiceUnavailable", "slow consumer") }
                    flusher.Flush()
                }
                return
            }
            kind := "MODIFIED"
            if ev.Type == "DELETE" { kind = "DELETED"
            } else if ev.PrevRev == 0 { kind = "ADDED" }
            if err := emitKVObject(w, info, kind, ev.Value, ev.Revision); err != nil { return }
            delivered = ev.Revision
            flusher.Flush()
        }
    }
}

func (s *APIServer) handleWatch(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, info *ResourceInfo, ns string) {
    prefix := storagePrefix(info, ns)
    query := r.URL.Query()
    initial := !query.Has("resourceVersion")
    startRev := int64(0)
    if !initial {
        var err error
        startRev, err = strconv.ParseInt(query.Get("resourceVersion"), 10, 64)
        if err != nil || startRev < 0 { http.Error(w, "invalid RV", 422); return }
    }
    flusher, ok := w.(http.Flusher)
    if !ok { http.Error(w, "no streaming", 500); return }
    var snapshot []kv.Pair
    var ch <-chan kv.Event
    var errs <-chan error
    var cancel func()
    for {
        if r.Context().Err() != nil { return }
        if initial { snapshot, startRev = s.kv.Range(prefix) }
        var err error
        ch, errs, cancel, err = s.kv.Watch(prefix, startRev)
        if initial && err == kv.ErrCompacted { continue }
        if err == kv.ErrCompacted { http.Error(w, "expired RV", 410); return }
        if err == kv.ErrSlowConsumer { http.Error(w, "slow consumer", 503); return }
        if err != nil { http.Error(w, err.Error(), 500); return }
        break
    }
    defer cancel()
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    w.WriteHeader(200)
    flusher.Flush()
    // emitKVObject 解码字节、投射所提供的 RV、写入 JSON + 换行，
    // 并返回任何解码/写入错误。其他 emit 辅助函数也返回错误。
    if initial {
        for _, item := range snapshot {
            if err := emitKVObject(w, info, "ADDED", item.Value, item.ModRevision); err != nil { return }
            flusher.Flush()
        }
        if err := emitBookmark(w, startRev); err != nil { return }
        flusher.Flush()
    }
    delivered := startRev
    ticker := time.NewTicker(30 * time.Second)
    defer ticker.Stop()
    for {
        select {
        case <-r.Context().Done(): return
        case <-ticker.C:
            if err := emitBookmark(w, delivered); err != nil { return }
            flusher.Flush()
        case ev, ok := <-ch:
            if !ok {
                if err, failed := <-errs; failed {
                    if err == kv.ErrCompacted { emitErr(w, 410, "Expired", "history compacted")
                    } else { emitErr(w, 503, "ServiceUnavailable", "slow consumer") }
                    flusher.Flush()
                }
                return
            }
            kind := "MODIFIED"
            if ev.Type == "DELETE" { kind = "DELETED"
            } else if ev.PrevRev == 0 { kind = "ADDED" }
            if err := emitKVObject(w, info, kind, ev.Value, ev.Revision); err != nil { return }
            delivered = ev.Revision
            flusher.Flush()
        }
    }
}
[28]

Keep stream writes bounded and cancelable, while allowing the overall Watch to remain open. The bookmark above reports the last event actually written, so it never advances past queued events.

保持流写入有界且可取消，同时允许整个 Watch 保持打开。上面的 bookmark 报告实际写入的最后一个事件，因此它永远不会越过已排队的事件。

[29]

10.3 The 410 Gone Contract

10.3 410 Gone 契约

[30]

Chapter 05 explains why this matters. If a client falls behind (its resourceVersion is older than the apiserver's compaction point), there is no way to deliver events from [oldRV, currentRV) because they have been dropped. The protocol is:

第 05 章解释了为什么这重要。如果客户端落后（其 resourceVersion 比 apiserver 的压缩点更旧），就没有办法投递 [oldRV, currentRV) 的事件，因为它们已经被丢弃。协议是：

   Client       Apiserver
     │ GET .../pods?watch=true&resourceVersion=12345
     ▼
                Look up watch from rev 12345
                If compactRev > 12345 → 410 before opening the stream
     ◄────────  ERROR event {code: 410}
     │ Connection stays open until client closes it (or closes immediately
     │ depending on impl). Client recovers by:
     │
     ▼
   1. LIST the resource at the current RV
   2. Reconcile local cache: items not in LIST → delete; items in LIST → upsert
   3. WATCH from the LIST's resourceVersion

   客户端       Apiserver
     │ GET .../pods?watch=true&resourceVersion=12345
     ▼
                查找从 rev 12345 开始的 watch
                如果 compactRev > 12345 → 在打开流之前返回 410
     ◄────────  ERROR 事件 {code: 410}
     │ 连接保持打开，直到客户端关闭它（或立即关闭，
     │ 取决于实现）。客户端通过以下方式恢复：
     │
     ▼
   1. 在当前 RV 处 LIST 该资源
   2. 协调本地缓存：不在 LIST 中的条目 → 删除；在 LIST 中的条目 → upsert
   3. 从 LIST 的 resourceVersion 开始 WATCH
[31]

The informer in chapter 08 implements this exact recovery loop. Our mini-informer will too.

第 08 章的 informer 实现了这个确切的恢复循环。我们的 mini-informer 也会。

10.4 Bookmarks

10.4 书签

Every ~30s we send a BOOKMARK event whose only payload is the current resourceVersion. The reason: a client that's been idle for 10 minutes (no events on its prefix) needs to advance its RV so that when it eventually reconnects, it doesn't ask for an RV that's been compacted. Bookmarks let watchers progress without traffic.

每隔约 30 秒我们发送一个 BOOKMARK 事件，其唯一负载是当前的 resourceVersion。原因是：一个已空闲 10 分钟的客户端（其前缀上没有事件）需要推进其 RV，以便当它最终重新连接时，不会请求一个已被压缩的 RV。书签让 watch 者可以在没有流量时也前进。

Chapter 05 §watch-bookmarks describes how this saves enormous amounts of work at scale: a controller watching Events in a quiet cluster would otherwise have its RV stay frozen and need to re-list on every reconnect.

第 05 章 §watch-bookmarks 描述了这在规模上如何节省巨量工作：在安静集群中 watch Events 的控制器，否则其 RV 会一直冻结，并需要在每次重新连接时重新列表。

11. Phase 6 — mini-kubelet: The Node Agent

11. 阶段 6 — mini-kubelet：节点代理

Now we cross back to the node. The kubelet is the only component that runs containers; everything else is a controller that scribbles objects.

现在我们跨回节点。kubelet 是唯一运行容器的组件；其他一切都是在对象上涂写的控制器。

11.1 The syncLoop

11.1 syncLoop

The kubelet has one main loop that consumes pod state changes from several sources, merges them, and reconciles to the runtime.

kubelet 有一个主循环，从多个来源消费 pod 状态变化，合并它们，并协调到运行时。

                       ┌────────────────────────────────┐
                       │     syncLoop (single goroutine)│
                       └──────────────┬─────────────────┘
                                      │
        ┌─────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┐
        ▼                             ▼                              ▼
   ┌──────────┐               ┌──────────────┐               ┌──────────────┐
   │ apiserver│               │ PLEG (runtime│               │ probe results│
   │  watch   │               │  state poll) │               │ (readiness,  │
   │  events  │               │              │               │  liveness)   │
   └──────────┘               └──────────────┘               └──────────────┘
        │                             │                              │
        └────────────┬────────────────┴──────────────┬───────────────┘
                     ▼                               ▼
              ┌────────────┐                  ┌──────────────┐
              │ pod worker │ ── per pod ──►   │ pod worker   │
              └─────┬──────┘                  └──────┬───────┘
                    │                                │
                    ▼ syncPod(pod)                   ▼
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ 1. ensure sandbox (RunPodSandbox if needed)    │
              │ 2. ensure each container (Create+Start)        │
              │ 3. write pod.status back via apiserver         │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

                       ┌────────────────────────────────┐
                       │   syncLoop（单个 goroutine）   │
                       └──────────────┬─────────────────┘
                                      │
        ┌─────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┐
        ▼                             ▼                              ▼
   ┌──────────┐               ┌──────────────┐               ┌──────────────┐
   │ apiserver│               │ PLEG（运行时 │               │ 探针结果     │
   │  watch   │               │  状态轮询）  │               │ （就绪、     │
   │   事件   │               │              │               │ 存活）       │
   └──────────┘               └──────────────┘               └──────────────┘
        │                             │                              │
        └────────────┬────────────────┴──────────────┬───────────────┘
                     ▼                               ▼
              ┌────────────┐                  ┌──────────────┐
              │ Pod 工作器 │ ── 每个 pod ──►  │ Pod 工作器   │
              └─────┬──────┘                  └──────┬───────┘
                    │                                │
                    ▼ syncPod(pod)                   ▼
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ 1. 确保沙箱（必要时 RunPodSandbox）            │
              │ 2. 确保每个容器（Create+Start）                │
              │ 3. 通过 apiserver 写回 pod.status              │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

This is chapter 10 in one diagram. The PLEG (Pod Lifecycle Event Generator) is a goroutine that polls the runtime every second, computes a diff against the last poll, and emits events of the form (podID, containerID, oldState, newState). The pod worker for that pod consumes those events and recomputes the desired actions.

这就是一张图里的第 10 章。PLEG（Pod 生命周期事件生成器）是一个 goroutine，它每秒轮询运行时，与上一次轮询计算差异，并发出形如 (podID, containerID, oldState, newState) 的事件。该 pod 的 pod worker 消费这些事件并重新计算期望的动作。

11.2 The Pod Cache and Pod Worker

11.2 Pod 缓存与 Pod Worker

// File: minik8s/kubelet/kubelet.go
type Kubelet struct {
    nodeName    string
    apiserver   *Client          // typed client to mini-apiserver
    runtime     cri.RuntimeClient
    cni         *CNIInvoker      // exec'd binary
    podCache    *PodCache        // desired state, indexed by UID
    runtimeCache *RuntimeCache   // last-observed runtime state from PLEG
    workers     map[types.UID]*podWorker
    statusMgr   *StatusManager
    probeMgr    *ProbeManager
}

type podWorker struct {
    pod        *Pod
    inbox      chan workEntry
    syncing    bool
}

type workEntry struct {
    kind   string // "sync" | "kill" | "update"
    update *Pod
}

func (k *Kubelet) Run() {
    // 1. Watch Pods scoped to this node.
    go k.watchAssignedPods()
    // 2. Run PLEG.
    go k.runPLEG()
    // 3. Run the status manager (batches status updates to apiserver).
    go k.statusMgr.Run()
    // 4. Run the probe manager.
    go k.probeMgr.Run()
    // 5. syncLoop fans events to per-pod workers.
    for {
        select {
        case ev := <-k.podCache.events:
            k.dispatch(ev.uid, workEntry{kind: "update", update: ev.pod})
        case ev := <-k.runtimeCache.events:
            // PLEG transition; re-sync the affected pod.
            k.dispatch(ev.podUID, workEntry{kind: "sync"})
        case ev := <-k.probeMgr.results:
            k.statusMgr.updateContainerReadiness(ev.podUID, ev.containerName, ev.ready)
        }
    }
}

func (k *Kubelet) dispatch(uid types.UID, w workEntry) {
    pw, ok := k.workers[uid]
    if !ok {
        pw = &podWorker{inbox: make(chan workEntry, 8)}
        k.workers[uid] = pw
        go k.runPodWorker(pw)
    }
    select {
    case pw.inbox <- w:
    default:
        // Coalesce: if a sync is already queued, no need to enqueue another.
    }
}

// 文件：minik8s/kubelet/kubelet.go
type Kubelet struct {
    nodeName    string
    apiserver   *Client          // 到 mini-apiserver 的类型化客户端
    runtime     cri.RuntimeClient
    cni         *CNIInvoker      // 被 exec 的二进制
    podCache    *PodCache        // 期望状态，按 UID 索引
    runtimeCache *RuntimeCache   // 来自 PLEG 的最后观察到的运行时状态
    workers     map[types.UID]*podWorker
    statusMgr   *StatusManager
    probeMgr    *ProbeManager
}

type podWorker struct {
    pod        *Pod
    inbox      chan workEntry
    syncing    bool
}

type workEntry struct {
    kind   string // "sync" | "kill" | "update"
    update *Pod
}

func (k *Kubelet) Run() {
    // 1. watch 限定到本节点的 Pods。
    go k.watchAssignedPods()
    // 2. 运行 PLEG。
    go k.runPLEG()
    // 3. 运行状态管理器（把状态更新批量发送到 apiserver）。
    go k.statusMgr.Run()
    // 4. 运行探针管理器。
    go k.probeMgr.Run()
    // 5. syncLoop 把事件扇出到每个 pod 的 worker。
    for {
        select {
        case ev := <-k.podCache.events:
            k.dispatch(ev.uid, workEntry{kind: "update", update: ev.pod})
        case ev := <-k.runtimeCache.events:
            // PLEG 状态转换；重新同步受影响的 pod。
            k.dispatch(ev.podUID, workEntry{kind: "sync"})
        case ev := <-k.probeMgr.results:
            k.statusMgr.updateContainerReadiness(ev.podUID, ev.containerName, ev.ready)
        }
    }
}

func (k *Kubelet) dispatch(uid types.UID, w workEntry) {
    pw, ok := k.workers[uid]
    if !ok {
        pw = &podWorker{inbox: make(chan workEntry, 8)}
        k.workers[uid] = pw
        go k.runPodWorker(pw)
    }
    select {
    case pw.inbox <- w:
    default:
        // 合并：如果已经排队了一个 sync，就无需再入队另一个。
    }
}

11.3 syncPod

11.3 syncPod

The heart of the kubelet:

kubelet 的心脏：

func (k *Kubelet) syncPod(pod *Pod) error {
    // 1. Look up runtime state.
    // Runtime lookup uses the Pod UID, including after kubelet restart.
    sandbox, err := k.runtime.GetSandboxByPodID(pod.UID)
    if err != nil && !isNotFound(err) { return err }
    runningContainers, err := k.runtime.ListContainers(pod.UID)
    if err != nil { return err }

    // 2. Sandbox: create if missing.
    if sandbox == nil {
        resp, err := k.runtime.RunPodSandbox(&cri.RunPodSandboxRequest{
            PodID: pod.UID, Namespace: pod.Namespace, Name: pod.Name,
            NsConfig: derivePodNs(pod),
        })
        if err != nil { return err }
        sandbox = &cri.Sandbox{ID: resp.PodSandboxID, NetnsPath: resp.NetnsPath}

    }
    // 3. Ensure network state and recover PodIP for an existing sandbox, too.
    // This is for the custom runtime. A CRI runtime owning CNI does this itself.
    podIP, err := k.cni.Ensure(pod, sandbox.NetnsPath)
    if err != nil { return err }
    pod.Status.PodIP = podIP

    // 4. Init containers are sequential; a successful one stays completed.
    have := indexByName(runningContainers)
    for _, c := range pod.Spec.InitContainers {
        state, err := k.ensureContainer(pod, sandbox, c, have[c.Name], true)
        if err != nil { return err }
        if state.State != "EXITED" || state.ExitCode != 0 {
            k.statusMgr.SetPodStatus(pod, statusFromFreshRuntime(pod))
            return nil // wait, retry a failed init, or report Never/Failed
        }
    }
    for _, c := range pod.Spec.Containers {
        if _, err := k.ensureContainer(pod, sandbox, c, have[c.Name], false); err != nil { return err }
    }
    desired := append(append([]Container(nil), pod.Spec.InitContainers...), pod.Spec.Containers...)

    // 5. Containers that are running but not in desired: stop & remove.
    desiredNames := nameSet(desired)
    for name, rc := range have {
        if !desiredNames[name] {
            k.runtime.StopContainer(rc.ID, 30)
            k.runtime.RemoveContainer(rc.ID)
        }
    }

    // 6. Update pod.status.
    k.statusMgr.SetPodStatus(pod, statusFromFreshRuntime(pod))
    return nil
}

func (k *Kubelet) syncPod(pod *Pod) error {
    // 1. 查找运行时状态。
    // 运行时查找使用 Pod UID，包括在 kubelet 重启之后。
    sandbox, err := k.runtime.GetSandboxByPodID(pod.UID)
    if err != nil && !isNotFound(err) { return err }
    runningContainers, err := k.runtime.ListContainers(pod.UID)
    if err != nil { return err }

    // 2. 沙箱：缺失则创建。
    if sandbox == nil {
        resp, err := k.runtime.RunPodSandbox(&cri.RunPodSandboxRequest{
            PodID: pod.UID, Namespace: pod.Namespace, Name: pod.Name,
            NsConfig: derivePodNs(pod),
        })
        if err != nil { return err }
        sandbox = &cri.Sandbox{ID: resp.PodSandboxID, NetnsPath: resp.NetnsPath}

    }
    // 3. 确保网络状态，并且对已存在的沙箱也恢复 PodIP。
    // 这是为自定义运行时准备的。拥有 CNI 的 CRI 运行时会自己做这件事。
    podIP, err := k.cni.Ensure(pod, sandbox.NetnsPath)
    if err != nil { return err }
    pod.Status.PodIP = podIP

    // 4. Init 容器是顺序的；成功的一个保持已完成。
    have := indexByName(runningContainers)
    for _, c := range pod.Spec.InitContainers {
        state, err := k.ensureContainer(pod, sandbox, c, have[c.Name], true)
        if err != nil { return err }
        if state.State != "EXITED" || state.ExitCode != 0 {
            k.statusMgr.SetPodStatus(pod, statusFromFreshRuntime(pod))
            return nil // 等待、重试失败的 init，或报告 Never/Failed
        }
    }
    for _, c := range pod.Spec.Containers {
        if _, err := k.ensureContainer(pod, sandbox, c, have[c.Name], false); err != nil { return err }
    }
    desired := append(append([]Container(nil), pod.Spec.InitContainers...), pod.Spec.Containers...)

    // 5. 正在运行但不在期望中的容器：停止并移除。
    desiredNames := nameSet(desired)
    for name, rc := range have {
        if !desiredNames[name] {
            k.runtime.StopContainer(rc.ID, 30)
            k.runtime.RemoveContainer(rc.ID)
        }
    }

    // 6. 更新 pod.status。
    k.statusMgr.SetPodStatus(pod, statusFromFreshRuntime(pod))
    return nil
}
[32]

ensureContainer first observes actual state: create missing containers, start Created containers, reuse Running containers, and apply restart policy to Exited containers. Successful init containers stay completed; failed init containers retry unless the policy is Never. For applications, Never keeps the result, OnFailure retries nonzero exits, and Always retries either exit code. Retried attempts use a bounded backoff. Check image availability and pull when missing before Create/Start; propagate errors, and use stable Pod UID/container/attempt labels to recover a lost response.

ensureContainer 首先观察实际状态：创建缺失的容器、启动 Created 状态的容器、复用 Running 状态的容器，并对 Exited 状态的容器应用重启策略。成功的 init 容器保持已完成；失败的 init 容器会重试，除非策略是 Never。对于应用容器，Never 保留结果，OnFailure 重试非零退出，Always 重试任意退出码。重试的尝试使用有界退避。在 Create/Start 之前检查镜像可用性并在缺失时拉取；传播错误，并使用稳定的 Pod UID/容器/尝试标签来恢复丢失的响应。

[33]

Ensure checks existing network state and repairs or retries missing setup; it is not an unconditional second ADD over an already configured interface. Remove a deleted Pod's CNI state and sandbox in retryable steps. Register the Node and periodically report capacity/Ready; an API List failure keeps running containers intact. The runtime contract and existing experiments cover these omitted helpers.

Ensure 检查现有网络状态，并修复或重试缺失的设置；它不是对已配置接口无条件地第二次 ADD。以可重试的步骤移除已删除 Pod 的 CNI 状态和沙箱。注册 Node 并周期性报告容量/Ready；API List 失败会让运行中的容器保持完整。运行时契约 和 现有实验 涵盖了这些被省略的辅助函数。

[34]

This is the kubelet loop; several implementation details remain to be filled in. The real kubelet has thousands of lines because it also handles:

这就是 kubelet 循环；还有几个实现细节待填充。真实 kubelet 有数千行，因为它还处理：

[35]
  • Init container ordering (must complete before app containers start) — chapter 11.
  • Lifecycle hooks (preStop, postStart) — chapter 11.
  • Volume mounts (chapter 19).
  • Image pull (chapter 02).
  • CPU/Memory/Topology managers (chapter 21).
  • Device plugin allocation (chapter 21).
  • Probe execution (chapter 11).
  • Eviction (chapter 21).
  • Log rotation (chapter 30).
  • Init 容器排序（必须在应用容器启动之前完成）——第 11 章。
  • 生命周期钩子（preStop、postStart）——第 11 章。
  • 卷挂载（第 19 章）。
  • 镜像拉取（第 02 章）。
  • CPU/内存/拓扑管理器（第 21 章）。
  • 设备插件分配（第 21 章）。
  • 探针执行（第 11 章）。
  • 驱逐（第 21 章）。
  • 日志轮转（第 30 章）。

Each of those is additive. Drop them in and syncPod keeps the same shape.

这些每一项都是可加的。把它们放进去，syncPod 保持相同的形状。

11.4 PLEG

11.4 PLEG

// File: minik8s/kubelet/pleg.go
type PLEG struct {
    runtime  cri.RuntimeClient
    lastObs  map[string]map[string]string // podID → containerID → state
    interval time.Duration
    events   chan plegEvent
}

func (p *PLEG) Run() {
    for range time.Tick(p.interval) {
        pods, _ := p.runtime.ListPodSandbox()
        newObs := map[string]map[string]string{}
        for _, pod := range pods {
            containers, _ := p.runtime.ListContainers(pod.ID)
            states := map[string]string{}
            for _, c := range containers {
                states[c.ID] = c.State
            }
            newObs[pod.ID] = states
        }
        // Diff with lastObs and emit events.
        for podID := range unionPodIDs(p.lastObs, newObs) {
            states := newObs[podID]
            prev := p.lastObs[podID]
            for cid, st := range states {
                if prev[cid] != st {
                    p.events <- plegEvent{
                        podUID: podID, containerID: cid,
                        oldState: prev[cid], newState: st,
                    }
                }
            }
            // Containers gone.
            for cid := range prev {
                if _, ok := states[cid]; !ok {
                    p.events <- plegEvent{podUID: podID, containerID: cid, newState: "REMOVED"}
                }
            }
        }
        p.lastObs = newObs
    }
}

// 文件：minik8s/kubelet/pleg.go
type PLEG struct {
    runtime  cri.RuntimeClient
    lastObs  map[string]map[string]string // podID → containerID → 状态
    interval time.Duration
    events   chan plegEvent
}

func (p *PLEG) Run() {
    for range time.Tick(p.interval) {
        pods, _ := p.runtime.ListPodSandbox()
        newObs := map[string]map[string]string{}
        for _, pod := range pods {
            containers, _ := p.runtime.ListContainers(pod.ID)
            states := map[string]string{}
            for _, c := range containers {
                states[c.ID] = c.State
            }
            newObs[pod.ID] = states
        }
        // 与 lastObs 做差异并发出事件。
        for podID := range unionPodIDs(p.lastObs, newObs) {
            states := newObs[podID]
            prev := p.lastObs[podID]
            for cid, st := range states {
                if prev[cid] != st {
                    p.events <- plegEvent{
                        podUID: podID, containerID: cid,
                        oldState: prev[cid], newState: st,
                    }
                }
            }
            // 容器消失。
            for cid := range prev {
                if _, ok := states[cid]; !ok {
                    p.events <- plegEvent{podUID: podID, containerID: cid, newState: "REMOVED"}
                }
            }
        }
        p.lastObs = newObs
    }
}
[36]

The "relisting" pattern is exactly what real PLEG does. Chapter 10 §PLEG explains why this design (polling+diff) is preferred over event-driven (every CRI runtime would need to emit events; the OCI contract has none). The downside is the 1-second polling latency before the kubelet notices that a container crashed.

“重新列表”模式正是真实 PLEG 所做的。第 10 章 §PLEG 解释了为什么这个设计（轮询+差异）优于事件驱动（每个 CRI 运行时都需要发出事件；OCI 契约没有事件）。缺点是在 kubelet 注意到容器崩溃之前有 1 秒的轮询延迟。

11.5 Status Manager

11.5 状态管理器

The kubelet batches status updates:

kubelet 批量处理 status 更新：

type StatusManager struct {
    pending map[types.UID]*PodStatus
    api     *Client
    mu      sync.Mutex
}

func (s *StatusManager) Run() {
    for range time.Tick(100 * time.Millisecond) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        batch := s.pending
        s.pending = map[types.UID]*PodStatus{}
        s.mu.Unlock()
        for uid, status := range batch {
            pod, _ := s.api.GetPodByUID(uid)
            if pod == nil { continue } // pod deleted
            if statusEqual(pod.Status, *status) { continue } // no change
            pod.Status = *status
            s.api.UpdatePodStatus(pod) // PATCH /api/v1/.../pods/<name>/status
        }
    }
}

type StatusManager struct {
    pending map[types.UID]*PodStatus
    api     *Client
    mu      sync.Mutex
}

func (s *StatusManager) Run() {
    for range time.Tick(100 * time.Millisecond) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        batch := s.pending
        s.pending = map[types.UID]*PodStatus{}
        s.mu.Unlock()
        for uid, status := range batch {
            pod, _ := s.api.GetPodByUID(uid)
            if pod == nil { continue } // pod 被删除
            if statusEqual(pod.Status, *status) { continue } // 无变化
            pod.Status = *status
            s.api.UpdatePodStatus(pod) // PATCH /api/v1/.../pods/<name>/status
        }
    }
}

The batching exists because real clusters have 30 status fields that flap every probe interval. Without batching, the apiserver gets one PATCH per probe per pod. With batching plus equality check, it gets one PATCH per meaningful change. Chapter 35 §apiserver-load explains why this is the single largest source of write load on a real apiserver.

批处理的存在是因为真实集群有 30 个状态字段，每个探针间隔都会抖动。没有批处理，apiserver 对每个 pod 的每个探针都会收到一个 PATCH。有了批处理加上相等性检查，它每次有意义的变化才收到一个 PATCH。第 35 章 §apiserver-load 解释了为什么这是真实 apiserver 上单一最大的写入负载来源。

11.6 What This Maps To

11.6 这映射到什么

The real kubelet is in pkg/kubelet/ and is ~150,000 lines. Our mini-kubelet is ~700. The 200× ratio is the missing managers (CPU/Memory/Topology/Device), volume manager (chapter 19), probe protocol implementations (HTTP, TCP, gRPC, exec — chapter 11), eviction with all its thresholds (chapter 21 §eviction), image GC (chapter 02 §image-gc), node lease management (chapter 04 §lease), the kubelet HTTP server for exec/attach/portforward (chapter 30 §kubelet-api), runtime version negotiation, feature gates, and ~30,000 lines of error handling for things that go wrong on real systems.

真实的 kubelet 位于 pkg/kubelet/，约有 150,000 行。我们的 mini-kubelet 约 700 行。200 倍的比率就来自那些缺失的管理器（CPU/内存/拓扑/设备）、卷管理器（第 19 章）、探针协议实现（HTTP、TCP、gRPC、exec——第 11 章）、带所有阈值的驱逐（第 21 章 §eviction）、镜像 GC（第 02 章 §image-gc）、节点租约管理（第 04 章 §lease）、用于 exec/attach/portforward 的 kubelet HTTP 服务器（第 30 章 §kubelet-api）、运行时版本协商、特性门控，以及约 30,000 行针对真实系统上出错情况的错误处理。

But the shape — syncLoop, PLEG, pod workers, status manager — is unchanged. If you read those four files in pkg/kubelet/ you will recognize every structure from our 700-line version.

但形状——syncLoop、PLEG、pod worker、状态管理器——没有改变。如果你读 pkg/kubelet/ 中的那四个文件，你会认出我们 700 行版本中的每一个结构。

12. Phase 7 — mini-scheduler: Filter, Score, Bind

12. 阶段 7 — mini-scheduler：过滤、评分、绑定

The scheduler is the simplest core component. It watches unbound pods, picks a node, and PATCHes the pod with spec.nodeName. That is the entire job.

调度器是最简单的核心组件。它 watch 未绑定的 pod，选择一个节点，并用 spec.nodeName PATCH 该 pod。这就是全部工作。

12.1 The Scheduling Cycle

12.1 调度周期

   Pod arrives with spec.nodeName == "":
       1. PreFilter: collect candidate set = all nodes
       2. Filter:    for each node, run feasibility checks
                       - NodeName matches if set
                       - Has enough CPU/memory (sum existing pods' requests)
                       - Tolerates taints (skipped in minik8s)
                     drop infeasible nodes
       3. Score:     for each remaining node, score 0..100
                       - least-allocated: prefer nodes with more free capacity
                     pick max score (break ties by name for determinism)
       4. Reserve:   speculatively account the pod's resources on chosen node
       5. Bind:      POST /api/v1/namespaces/<ns>/pods/<name>/binding
                     with target.name = chosen and Pod identity/version preconditions
                     (the handler changes only nodeName and still checks CAS;
                      concurrent status writes may require a re-read/retry)

   Pod 到达时 spec.nodeName == ""：
       1. PreFilter：收集候选集合 = 所有节点
       2. Filter：   对每个节点运行可行性检查
                       - 如果设置了 NodeName 则匹配
                       - 有足够的 CPU/内存（对现有 pod 的 requests 求和）
                       - 容忍污点（在 minik8s 中跳过）
                     丢弃不可行的节点
       3. Score：    对每个剩余节点评分 0..100
                       - 最少分配：偏好有更多空闲容量的节点
                     取最高分（为确定性按名字打破平局）
       4. Reserve：  推测性地在被选节点上计入该 pod 的资源
       5. Bind：      POST /api/v1/namespaces/<ns>/pods/<name>/binding
                     带 target.name = 被选中的节点以及 Pod 身份/版本前置条件
                     （处理器只改变 nodeName 且仍检查 CAS；
                      并发的状态写入可能需要重新读取/重试）
[37]

12.2 The Pseudocode

12.2 伪代码

// File: minik8s/scheduler/scheduler.go
type Scheduler struct {
    api       *Client
    podLister cache.Lister
    nodeLister cache.Lister
    queue     workqueue.RateLimitingInterface
    cache     *schedulerCache // running tally of resources per node
}

func (s *Scheduler) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    // Informers for pods and nodes.
    podInf := newPodInformer(s.api, "")
    nodeInf := newNodeInformer(s.api)
    podInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc: func(obj interface{}) {
            pod := obj.(*Pod)
            if pod.Spec.NodeName == "" {
                s.queue.Add(key(pod))
            }
        },
        UpdateFunc: func(old, new interface{}) {
            np := new.(*Pod)
            if np.Spec.NodeName == "" { s.queue.Add(key(np)) }
        },
    })
    go podInf.Run(ctx)
    go nodeInf.Run(ctx)
    podInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)
    nodeInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)

    // Worker.
    for {
        k, quit := s.queue.Get()
        if quit { return }
        if err := s.scheduleOne(k); err != nil {
            s.queue.AddRateLimited(k)
        } else {
            s.queue.Forget(k)
        }
        s.queue.Done(k)
    }
}

func (s *Scheduler) scheduleOne(podKey string) error {
    pod, err := s.podLister.Get(podKey)
    if err != nil || pod == nil { return nil }
    if pod.Spec.NodeName != "" { return nil } // already scheduled

    // 1. Snapshot nodes.
    nodes, _ := s.nodeLister.List()

    // 2. Filter.
    feasible := []*Node{}
    for _, n := range nodes {
        if !s.fits(pod, n) { continue }
        feasible = append(feasible, n)
    }
    if len(feasible) == 0 {
        s.recordEvent(pod, "FailedScheduling", "no feasible nodes")
        return errNoFit
    }

    // 3. Score.
    bestScore := int64(-1)
    var best *Node
    for _, n := range feasible {
        sc := s.score(pod, n)
        if sc > bestScore || (sc == bestScore && (best == nil || n.Name < best.Name)) {
            bestScore = sc
            best = n
        }
    }

    // 4. Reserve.
    s.cache.assume(pod, best.Name)

    // 5. Bind via subresource.
    binding := &Binding{
        Metadata: ObjectMeta{Name: pod.Name, Namespace: pod.Namespace},
        Target:   ObjectReference{Kind: "Node", Name: best.Name},
    }
    if err := s.api.CreateBinding(binding); err != nil {
        s.cache.forget(pod, best.Name)
        return err
    }
    return nil
}

func (s *Scheduler) fits(pod *Pod, node *Node) bool {
    if pod.Spec.NodeName != "" && pod.Spec.NodeName != node.Name { return false }
    cpu, memory := node.Status.Allocatable.CPU, node.Status.Allocatable.Memory
    if cpu <= 0 || memory <= 0 || !nodeReady(node) || node.Spec.Unschedulable { return false }
    used := s.cache.usedOnNode(node.Name)
    needCPU, needMem := totalRequests(pod)
    // Compare by subtraction after bounds checks to avoid overflow.
    return used.cpu >= 0 && used.cpu <= cpu && needCPU <= cpu-used.cpu &&
        used.memory >= 0 && used.memory <= memory && needMem <= memory-used.memory
}

func (s *Scheduler) score(pod *Pod, node *Node) int64 {
    // Least allocated: 100 - (utilization%).
    used := s.cache.usedOnNode(node.Name)
    // floorPercent uses an overflow-safe multiply/divide for nonnegative values.
    cpuUtil := floorPercent(used.cpu, node.Status.Allocatable.CPU)
    memUtil := floorPercent(used.memory, node.Status.Allocatable.Memory)
    return (200 - cpuUtil - memUtil) / 2
}

// 文件：minik8s/scheduler/scheduler.go
type Scheduler struct {
    api       *Client
    podLister cache.Lister
    nodeLister cache.Lister
    queue     workqueue.RateLimitingInterface
    cache     *schedulerCache // 每个节点资源的持续累计统计
}

func (s *Scheduler) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    // 用于 pod 和节点的 informer。
    podInf := newPodInformer(s.api, "")
    nodeInf := newNodeInformer(s.api)
    podInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc: func(obj interface{}) {
            pod := obj.(*Pod)
            if pod.Spec.NodeName == "" {
                s.queue.Add(key(pod))
            }
        },
        UpdateFunc: func(old, new interface{}) {
            np := new.(*Pod)
            if np.Spec.NodeName == "" { s.queue.Add(key(np)) }
        },
    })
    go podInf.Run(ctx)
    go nodeInf.Run(ctx)
    podInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)
    nodeInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)

    // worker。
    for {
        k, quit := s.queue.Get()
        if quit { return }
        if err := s.scheduleOne(k); err != nil {
            s.queue.AddRateLimited(k)
        } else {
            s.queue.Forget(k)
        }
        s.queue.Done(k)
    }
}

func (s *Scheduler) scheduleOne(podKey string) error {
    pod, err := s.podLister.Get(podKey)
    if err != nil || pod == nil { return nil }
    if pod.Spec.NodeName != "" { return nil } // 已调度

    // 1. 快照节点。
    nodes, _ := s.nodeLister.List()

    // 2. 过滤。
    feasible := []*Node{}
    for _, n := range nodes {
        if !s.fits(pod, n) { continue }
        feasible = append(feasible, n)
    }
    if len(feasible) == 0 {
        s.recordEvent(pod, "FailedScheduling", "no feasible nodes")
        return errNoFit
    }

    // 3. 评分。
    bestScore := int64(-1)
    var best *Node
    for _, n := range feasible {
        sc := s.score(pod, n)
        if sc > bestScore || (sc == bestScore && (best == nil || n.Name < best.Name)) {
            bestScore = sc
            best = n
        }
    }

    // 4. 预留。
    s.cache.assume(pod, best.Name)

    // 5. 通过子资源绑定。
    binding := &Binding{
        Metadata: ObjectMeta{Name: pod.Name, Namespace: pod.Namespace},
        Target:   ObjectReference{Kind: "Node", Name: best.Name},
    }
    if err := s.api.CreateBinding(binding); err != nil {
        s.cache.forget(pod, best.Name)
        return err
    }
    return nil
}

func (s *Scheduler) fits(pod *Pod, node *Node) bool {
    if pod.Spec.NodeName != "" && pod.Spec.NodeName != node.Name { return false }
    cpu, memory := node.Status.Allocatable.CPU, node.Status.Allocatable.Memory
    if cpu <= 0 || memory <= 0 || !nodeReady(node) || node.Spec.Unschedulable { return false }
    used := s.cache.usedOnNode(node.Name)
    needCPU, needMem := totalRequests(pod)
    // 在边界检查之后通过减法比较以避免溢出。
    return used.cpu >= 0 && used.cpu <= cpu && needCPU <= cpu-used.cpu &&
        used.memory >= 0 && used.memory <= memory && needMem <= memory-used.memory
}

func (s *Scheduler) score(pod *Pod, node *Node) int64 {
    // 最少分配：100 - (利用率%)。
    used := s.cache.usedOnNode(node.Name)
    // floorPercent 对非负值使用防溢出的乘法/除法。
    cpuUtil := floorPercent(used.cpu, node.Status.Allocatable.CPU)
    memUtil := floorPercent(used.memory, node.Status.Allocatable.Memory)
    return (200 - cpuUtil - memUtil) / 2
}
[38]

Before scheduling, build the cache from a List of bound, nonterminal Pods. Update it by UID on add/update/delete, reconcile an assumed Pod with its observed binding without counting twice, and release resources when a Pod is deleted or terminates. If a binding response is lost, re-read the Pod before forgetting its reservation. CPU uses milliCPU and memory uses bytes; totalRequests takes the greater of the application sum and the largest sequential init request, per resource. Run one scheduler/worker here. The reference uses fresh API Lists instead of a second Assume ledger; the scheduler contract explains this simpler choice.

在调度之前，从一个已绑定、非终止 Pod 的 List 构建缓存。在添加/更新/删除时按 UID 更新它，把已假设的 Pod 与其观察到的绑定协调而不重复计算，并在 Pod 被删除或终止时释放资源。如果绑定响应丢失，在忘记其预留之前重新读取该 Pod。CPU 使用 milliCPU，内存使用字节；totalRequests 对每种资源取应用求和与最大的顺序 init 请求中的较大者。这里运行一个调度器/worker。参考实现使用新的 API List 而不是第二份 Assume 账本；调度器契约 解释了这个更简单的选择。

[39]

12.3 The Binding Subresource

12.3 绑定子资源

[40]

On the apiserver side we need a new route:

在 apiserver 侧我们需要一个新路由：

POST /api/v1/namespaces/{ns}/pods/{name}/binding
   body: {"target": {"kind": "Node", "name": "node-1"}}
   semantics: server PATCHes spec.nodeName, does not return the pod

POST /api/v1/namespaces/{ns}/pods/{name}/binding
   请求体：{"target": {"kind": "Node", "name": "node-1"}}
   语义：服务器 PATCH spec.nodeName，不返回该 pod

This is exactly how real Kubernetes does it. The reason it's a subresource: a bind action is conceptually different from a generic update to the pod (different RBAC, different validation, atomic write to one specific field). Chapter 09 §binding-vs-update covers the rationale.

真实 Kubernetes 正是这么做的。它是子资源的原因：bind 动作在概念上不同于对 pod 的通用 update（不同的 RBAC、不同的校验、对某一个特定字段的原子写入）。第 09 章 §binding-vs-update 涵盖了其理由。

12.4 What We Don't Do

12.4 我们不做什么

The real scheduler has the Scheduler Framework (chapter 09) with ~20 plugin types and ~30 built-in plugins: NodeAffinity, NodePorts, VolumeBinding, PodTopologySpread, InterPodAffinity, TaintToleration, ImageLocality, etc. We have two: feasibility-by-resources and score-by-least-allocated. Each real plugin is ~200-500 lines and slots into the same fits/score extension points we have. Adding TaintToleration to mini-scheduler is a one-day project: add tolerations to the pod spec, taints to the node spec, evaluate during fits.

真实调度器有 Scheduler Framework（第 09 章），有约 20 种插件类型和约 30 个内置插件：NodeAffinity、NodePorts、VolumeBinding、PodTopologySpread、InterPodAffinity、TaintToleration、ImageLocality 等。我们有两个：按资源判断可行性、按最少分配评分。每个真实插件约 200-500 行，并接入我们拥有的相同 fits/score 扩展点。把 TaintToleration 加入 mini-scheduler 是一天的项目：向 pod spec 添加 tolerations，向 node spec 添加 taints，在 fits 期间求值。

Preemption (chapter 09 §preemption), gang scheduling (chapter 34 §volcano), scheduling gates (chapter 09 §gates), the descheduler (chapter 09 §descheduler) — all are layered on. None changes the fundamental loop.

抢占（第 09 章 §preemption）、gang 调度（第 34 章 §volcano）、调度门控（第 09 章 §gates）、descheduler（第 09 章 §descheduler）——全都是叠加在其上的。没有一个会改变基础循环。

13. Phase 8 — mini-controller-manager: Informers and Reconcilers

13. 阶段 8 — mini-controller-manager：Informer 与协调器

The controller manager is multiple controllers in one binary. Each controller follows the same pattern: list+watch, cache, workqueue, reconcile. We build the pattern library once and instantiate it three times: Deployment, ReplicaSet, Endpoints.

控制器管理器是在一个二进制文件中包含多个控制器。每个控制器都遵循相同的模式：list+watch、缓存、工作队列、协调。我们只构建一次这个模式库，然后将它实例化三次：Deployment、ReplicaSet、Endpoints。

13.1 The Informer

13.1 Informer

// File: minik8s/lib/informer/informer.go
type Informer struct {
    lister   func(context.Context) ([]Object, string, error)
    watcher  func(context.Context, string) (<-chan Event, func(), error) // stop cancels and joins the stream
    cache    *ThreadSafeStore                          // namespace/name → object
    handlers []ResourceEventHandler
    indexer  *Indexer                                  // optional secondary indices
    rvLatest string
    synced   chan struct{}
    syncOnce sync.Once
}

type ResourceEventHandler interface {
    OnAdd(obj Object)
    OnUpdate(old, new Object)
    OnDelete(obj Object)
}

func (inf *Informer) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    for ctx.Err() == nil {
        objs, rv, err := inf.lister(ctx)
        if err != nil { if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }; continue }
        // Install copies atomically, then emit the old/new diff outside the cache lock.
        // UID change at one key is delete-old + add-new. Unchanged RV emits nothing.
        inf.replaceAndNotify(objs)
        inf.rvLatest = rv
        inf.syncOnce.Do(func() { close(inf.synced) })
        ch, stop, err := inf.watcher(ctx, rv)
        if err != nil { if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }; continue }
        func() {
            defer stop()
            for {
                select {
                case <-ctx.Done(): return
                case ev, ok := <-ch:
                    if !ok || ev.Type == "ERROR" { return }
                    if ev.Type == "BOOKMARK" { inf.rvLatest = rvOf(ev.Object); continue }
                    // Validate key/UID/RV, install a deep copy, then notify with copies.
                    inf.applyAndNotify(ev)
                    inf.rvLatest = rvOf(ev.Object)
                }
            }
        }()
        if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }
    }
}

// 文件： minik8s/lib/informer/informer.go
type Informer struct {
    lister   func(context.Context) ([]Object, string, error)
    watcher  func(context.Context, string) (<-chan Event, func(), error) // stop 取消流并等待流结束
    cache    *ThreadSafeStore                          // namespace/name → 对象
    handlers []ResourceEventHandler
    indexer  *Indexer                                  // 可选的辅助索引
    rvLatest string
    synced   chan struct{}
    syncOnce sync.Once
}

type ResourceEventHandler interface {
    OnAdd(obj Object)
    OnUpdate(old, new Object)
    OnDelete(obj Object)
}

func (inf *Informer) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    for ctx.Err() == nil {
        objs, rv, err := inf.lister(ctx)
        if err != nil { if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }; continue }
        // 原子地安装副本，然后在缓存锁之外发出新旧差异。
        // 一个键上的 UID 变化就是删除旧对象 + 添加新对象。RV 不变则不发出任何通知。
        inf.replaceAndNotify(objs)
        inf.rvLatest = rv
        inf.syncOnce.Do(func() { close(inf.synced) })
        ch, stop, err := inf.watcher(ctx, rv)
        if err != nil { if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }; continue }
        func() {
            defer stop()
            for {
                select {
                case <-ctx.Done(): return
                case ev, ok := <-ch:
                    if !ok || ev.Type == "ERROR" { return }
                    if ev.Type == "BOOKMARK" { inf.rvLatest = rvOf(ev.Object); continue }
                    // 校验键/UID/RV，安装深拷贝，然后用副本通知。
                    inf.applyAndNotify(ev)
                    inf.rvLatest = rvOf(ev.Object)
                }
            }
        }()
        if !waitRetry(ctx) { return }
    }
}
[41]

The reconnect loop retains the cache while List fails and applies a diff after List succeeds: new keys produce OnAdd, changed keys OnUpdate, missing keys OnDelete. Initialize synced once; close it once after installing the first List and its notifications. waitRetry is a bounded retry delay selected together with ctx.Done. Cache reads, event callbacks and replacements use copies. The simple loop above re-lists after a disconnect; resuming the last applied RV after ordinary EOF is an alternative, with 410/invalid events still returning to List. Every old stream is stopped first.

重连循环在 List 失败时保留缓存，并在 List 成功后应用差异： 新键产生 OnAdd，变化的键产生 OnUpdate，缺失的键产生 OnDelete。初始化 synced 一次； 在安装首次 List 的结果及完成其通知后关闭它一次。waitRetry 是一个有界的重试 延迟，与 ctx.Done 一起进行 select。缓存读取、事件回调和替换都使用副本。 上面的简单循环在断开连接后重新执行 List；另一种选择是在普通 EOF 后从最后应用的 RV 恢复，而遇到 410/无效事件时仍返回 List。每个旧流都会先被停止。

[42]

13.2 The Workqueue

13.2 工作队列

[43]
// File: minik8s/lib/workqueue/queue.go
type RateLimitingInterface struct {
    queue       []string
    dirty       map[string]struct{}   // keys waiting to be processed
    processing  map[string]struct{}   // keys currently being processed
    delayed     map[string]time.Time  // key → next-eligible time
    rateLimit   map[string]int        // exponential backoff counter
    timers      map[string]*time.Timer
    shutdown    bool
    mu          sync.Mutex
    cond        *sync.Cond
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Add(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    if q.shutdown { return }
    if timer := q.timers[key]; timer != nil { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    delete(q.delayed, key)
    q.addLocked(key)
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) addLocked(key string) {
    if _, ok := q.dirty[key]; ok { return } // coalesce
    if _, ok := q.processing[key]; ok {
        q.dirty[key] = struct{}{}
        return // will re-queue after Done
    }
    q.dirty[key] = struct{}{}
    q.queue = append(q.queue, key)
    q.cond.Signal()
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) AddRateLimited(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    if q.shutdown { return }
    retries := q.rateLimit[key]
    if retries < 63 { q.rateLimit[key]++ }
    delay := time.Second
    for i := 0; i < retries && delay < 60*time.Second; i++ { delay *= 2 }
    if delay > 60*time.Second { delay = 60*time.Second }
    deadline := time.Now().Add(delay)
    if old, ok := q.delayed[key]; ok && !deadline.Before(old) { return }
    if old := q.timers[key]; old != nil { old.Stop() }
    q.delayed[key] = deadline
    var timer *time.Timer
    timer = time.AfterFunc(delay, func() {
        q.mu.Lock()
        defer q.mu.Unlock()
        if q.shutdown || q.timers[key] != timer { return }
        delete(q.timers, key)
        delete(q.delayed, key)
        q.addLocked(key)
    })
    q.timers[key] = timer
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Get() (string, bool) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    for len(q.queue) == 0 && !q.shutdown { q.cond.Wait() }
    if q.shutdown { return "", true }
    key := q.queue[0]
    q.queue = q.queue[1:]
    delete(q.dirty, key)
    q.processing[key] = struct{}{}
    return key, false
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Done(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    delete(q.processing, key)
    if _, ok := q.dirty[key]; ok && !q.shutdown {
        // Re-enqueue: a new event arrived during processing.
        q.queue = append(q.queue, key)
        q.cond.Signal()
    }
    q.mu.Unlock()
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Forget(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock(); defer q.mu.Unlock()
    delete(q.rateLimit, key)
    if timer := q.timers[key]; timer != nil { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    delete(q.delayed, key)
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) ShutDown() {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    q.shutdown = true
    for key, timer := range q.timers { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    clear(q.delayed)
    q.queue = nil
    q.cond.Broadcast()
}

// 文件： minik8s/lib/workqueue/queue.go
type RateLimitingInterface struct {
    queue       []string
    dirty       map[string]struct{}   // 等待处理的键
    processing  map[string]struct{}   // 当前正在处理的键
    delayed     map[string]time.Time  // 键 → 下次可处理的时间
    rateLimit   map[string]int        // 指数退避计数器
    timers      map[string]*time.Timer
    shutdown    bool
    mu          sync.Mutex
    cond        *sync.Cond
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Add(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    if q.shutdown { return }
    if timer := q.timers[key]; timer != nil { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    delete(q.delayed, key)
    q.addLocked(key)
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) addLocked(key string) {
    if _, ok := q.dirty[key]; ok { return } // 合并
    if _, ok := q.processing[key]; ok {
        q.dirty[key] = struct{}{}
        return // 将在 Done 后重新入队
    }
    q.dirty[key] = struct{}{}
    q.queue = append(q.queue, key)
    q.cond.Signal()
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) AddRateLimited(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    if q.shutdown { return }
    retries := q.rateLimit[key]
    if retries < 63 { q.rateLimit[key]++ }
    delay := time.Second
    for i := 0; i < retries && delay < 60*time.Second; i++ { delay *= 2 }
    if delay > 60*time.Second { delay = 60*time.Second }
    deadline := time.Now().Add(delay)
    if old, ok := q.delayed[key]; ok && !deadline.Before(old) { return }
    if old := q.timers[key]; old != nil { old.Stop() }
    q.delayed[key] = deadline
    var timer *time.Timer
    timer = time.AfterFunc(delay, func() {
        q.mu.Lock()
        defer q.mu.Unlock()
        if q.shutdown || q.timers[key] != timer { return }
        delete(q.timers, key)
        delete(q.delayed, key)
        q.addLocked(key)
    })
    q.timers[key] = timer
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Get() (string, bool) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    for len(q.queue) == 0 && !q.shutdown { q.cond.Wait() }
    if q.shutdown { return "", true }
    key := q.queue[0]
    q.queue = q.queue[1:]
    delete(q.dirty, key)
    q.processing[key] = struct{}{}
    return key, false
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Done(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock()
    delete(q.processing, key)
    if _, ok := q.dirty[key]; ok && !q.shutdown {
        // 重新入队：处理期间有新事件到达。
        q.queue = append(q.queue, key)
        q.cond.Signal()
    }
    q.mu.Unlock()
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) Forget(key string) {
    q.mu.Lock(); defer q.mu.Unlock()
    delete(q.rateLimit, key)
    if timer := q.timers[key]; timer != nil { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    delete(q.delayed, key)
}

func (q *RateLimitingInterface) ShutDown() {
    q.mu.Lock()
    defer q.mu.Unlock()
    q.shutdown = true
    for key, timer := range q.timers { timer.Stop(); delete(q.timers, key) }
    clear(q.delayed)
    q.queue = nil
    q.cond.Broadcast()
}
[44]

Initialize every map and q.cond = sync.NewCond(&q.mu) in the queue constructor. Each worker's owner calls ShutDown when ctx is canceled; Get returns quit=true, matching the worker loops. A successful Get always has a Done, including failed reconciles. Canceling a timer also removes its identity from the map, so an already-started old callback cannot enqueue a replacement timer's key.

在队列构造函数中初始化每个 map 和 q.cond = sync.NewCond(&q.mu)。每个工作线程的 拥有者在 ctx 被取消时调用 ShutDown；Get 返回 quit=true，与工作线程循环相匹配。 成功的 Get 总会有一个 Done，包括协调失败时。取消计时器也会从 map 中移除 它的标识，因此已经启动的旧回调无法将替代计时器的键加入队列。

[45]

The four invariants: 1. Coalescing: if a key is enqueued twice before processing, only one entry exists. 2. Mutex-with-processing: a key cannot be processed twice concurrently; if events arrive during processing, they're held in dirty and re-enqueued on Done. 3. Rate limiting: failed reconciles retry with exponential backoff (AddRateLimited). 4. Forget on success: clear backoff state.

四个不变量： 1. 合并：如果一个键在处理前入队两次，队列中只存在一个条目。 2. 处理互斥：一个键无法被同时处理两次；如果事件在处理期间到达，它们会保留在 dirty 中，并在 Done 时重新入队。 3. 限速：失败的协调使用指数退避进行重试（AddRateLimited）。 4. 成功后遗忘：清除退避状态。

[46]

These are exactly the invariants of client-go/util/workqueue. Chapter 08 §workqueue covers them line by line.

这些恰好就是 client-go/util/workqueue 的不变量。第 08 章 §workqueue 逐行讲解了它们。

13.3 The Reconciler

13.3 协调器

A controller is now ~50 lines:

现在，一个控制器大约有 50 行：

// File: minik8s/controller/replicaset/controller.go
type ReplicaSetController struct {
    rsLister  cache.Lister
    podLister cache.Lister
    api       *Client
    queue     workqueue.RateLimitingInterface
}

func (c *ReplicaSetController) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    // Wire informers — both RS and Pod (pods may be deleted out from under us).
    rsInf := newReplicaSetInformer(c.api)
    podInf := newPodInformer(c.api, "")

    rsInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc:    func(o interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(o)) },
        UpdateFunc: func(_, n interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(n)) },
        DeleteFunc: func(o interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(o)) },
    })
    // Pod events: if a pod belongs to one of our RS (via ownerRef), enqueue
    // that RS for re-reconcile.
    podInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc:    func(o interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(o.(*Pod)) },
        UpdateFunc: func(_, n interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(n.(*Pod)) },
        DeleteFunc: func(o interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(o.(*Pod)) },
    })

    go rsInf.Run(ctx); go podInf.Run(ctx)
    rsInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx); podInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)

    for {
        k, quit := c.queue.Get()
        if quit { return }
        if err := c.reconcile(k); err != nil {
            c.queue.AddRateLimited(k)
        } else {
            c.queue.Forget(k)
        }
        c.queue.Done(k)
    }
}

func (c *ReplicaSetController) reconcile(rsKey string) error {
    rs, _ := c.rsLister.Get(rsKey)
    if rs == nil { return nil } // deleted

    // 1. Find all pods matching this RS's selector.
    allPods, err := c.api.ListPods(rs.Namespace) // committed writes count on the next pass
    if err != nil { return err }
    owned := []*Pod{}
    for _, p := range allPods {
        if isOwnedBy(p, rs) && labelsMatch(p.Labels, rs.Spec.Selector) {
            owned = append(owned, p)
        }
    }

    // 2. Filter out terminating.
    alive := []*Pod{}
    for _, p := range owned {
        if p.DeletionTimestamp == nil && p.Status.Phase != "Succeeded" && p.Status.Phase != "Failed" {
            alive = append(alive, p)
        }
    }

    // 3. Diff against desired.
    desired := rs.Spec.Replicas
    actual := int32(len(alive))
    diff := desired - actual

    switch {
    case diff > 0:
        // Create pods.
        for i := int32(0); i < diff; i++ {
            pod := buildPodFromTemplate(rs)
            if err := c.api.CreatePod(pod); err != nil { return err }
        }
    case diff < 0:
        // Delete excess pods. Pick the youngest first (or unhealthy first).
        sort.Slice(alive, func(i, j int) bool {
            return alive[i].CreationTimestamp.After(alive[j].CreationTimestamp)
        })
        for i := int32(0); i < -diff; i++ {
            if err := c.api.DeletePod(alive[i]); err != nil { return err }
        }
    }

    // 4. Update RS status.
    observed, err := c.api.ListOwnedActivePods(rs) // namespace + owner UID + selector
    if err != nil { return err }
    next := rs.DeepCopy()
    next.Status.Replicas = int32(len(observed))
    next.Status.ReadyReplicas = countReady(observed)
    next.Status.AvailableReplicas = countAvailable(observed)
    if statusEqual(rs.Status, next.Status) { return nil }
    return c.api.UpdateReplicaSetStatus(next)
}

// 文件： minik8s/controller/replicaset/controller.go
type ReplicaSetController struct {
    rsLister  cache.Lister
    podLister cache.Lister
    api       *Client
    queue     workqueue.RateLimitingInterface
}

func (c *ReplicaSetController) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    // 接入 Informer——RS 和 Pod 都要接入（Pod 可能在我们不知情时被删除）。
    rsInf := newReplicaSetInformer(c.api)
    podInf := newPodInformer(c.api, "")

    rsInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc:    func(o interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(o)) },
        UpdateFunc: func(_, n interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(n)) },
        DeleteFunc: func(o interface{}) { c.queue.Add(key(o)) },
    })
    // Pod 事件：如果一个 Pod 属于我们的某个 RS（通过 ownerRef），则将
    // 该 RS 入队，以再次协调。
    podInf.AddEventHandler(cache.ResourceEventHandlerFuncs{
        AddFunc:    func(o interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(o.(*Pod)) },
        UpdateFunc: func(_, n interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(n.(*Pod)) },
        DeleteFunc: func(o interface{}) { c.enqueueOwnerRS(o.(*Pod)) },
    })

    go rsInf.Run(ctx); go podInf.Run(ctx)
    rsInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx); podInf.WaitForCacheSync(ctx)

    for {
        k, quit := c.queue.Get()
        if quit { return }
        if err := c.reconcile(k); err != nil {
            c.queue.AddRateLimited(k)
        } else {
            c.queue.Forget(k)
        }
        c.queue.Done(k)
    }
}

func (c *ReplicaSetController) reconcile(rsKey string) error {
    rs, _ := c.rsLister.Get(rsKey)
    if rs == nil { return nil } // 已删除

    // 1. 找出所有匹配此 RS 选择器的 Pod。
    allPods, err := c.api.ListPods(rs.Namespace) // 已提交的写入在下一轮计入
    if err != nil { return err }
    owned := []*Pod{}
    for _, p := range allPods {
        if isOwnedBy(p, rs) && labelsMatch(p.Labels, rs.Spec.Selector) {
            owned = append(owned, p)
        }
    }

    // 2. 过滤掉正在终止的对象。
    alive := []*Pod{}
    for _, p := range owned {
        if p.DeletionTimestamp == nil && p.Status.Phase != "Succeeded" && p.Status.Phase != "Failed" {
            alive = append(alive, p)
        }
    }

    // 3. 计算与期望值的差异。
    desired := rs.Spec.Replicas
    actual := int32(len(alive))
    diff := desired - actual

    switch {
    case diff > 0:
        // 创建 Pod。
        for i := int32(0); i < diff; i++ {
            pod := buildPodFromTemplate(rs)
            if err := c.api.CreatePod(pod); err != nil { return err }
        }
    case diff < 0:
        // 删除多余的 Pod。优先选择最年轻的（或优先选择不健康的）。
        sort.Slice(alive, func(i, j int) bool {
            return alive[i].CreationTimestamp.After(alive[j].CreationTimestamp)
        })
        for i := int32(0); i < -diff; i++ {
            if err := c.api.DeletePod(alive[i]); err != nil { return err }
        }
    }

    // 4. 更新 RS 状态。
    observed, err := c.api.ListOwnedActivePods(rs) // 命名空间 + 拥有者 UID + 选择器
    if err != nil { return err }
    next := rs.DeepCopy()
    next.Status.Replicas = int32(len(observed))
    next.Status.ReadyReplicas = countReady(observed)
    next.Status.AvailableReplicas = countAvailable(observed)
    if statusEqual(rs.Status, next.Status) { return nil }
    return c.api.UpdateReplicaSetStatus(next)
}
[47]

Validate template labels against the selector at the API. isOwnedBy compares kind/name/UID and namespace; template materialization copies the template and assigns fresh identity. Use stable owner-UID/slot names for create retries with uncertain responses, or an expectations ledger when reading from cache. Observe committed creations before deciding how many more to create. A status write uses RV and skips unchanged state. Pod updates enqueue both old and new owners.

在 API 处根据选择器校验模板标签。isOwnedBy 比较 kind/name/UID 和 命名空间；模板实例化会复制模板并分配新的身份。对于响应不确定的创建重试，使用稳定的 拥有者 UID/槽位名称，或者在从缓存读取时使用预期账本。 在决定还要创建多少个之前，先观察已提交的创建结果。状态 写入使用 RV，并跳过未变化的状态。Pod 更新会将新旧拥有者都加入队列。

[48]

Read this carefully. Every line maps to a real K8s concept:

仔细阅读这段代码。每一行都对应一个真实的 K8s 概念：

[49]
  • isOwnedBy(p, rs) — owner references (chapter 36).
  • labelsMatch — label selector evaluation (chapter 05).
  • DeletionTimestamp == nil — exclude terminating pods (chapter 36).
  • buildPodFromTemplate(rs) — Spec.Template materialization (chapter 12).
  • Status update separate from spec update — the spec/status discipline (chapter 23).
  • isOwnedBy(p, rs)——拥有者引用（第 36 章）。
  • labelsMatch——标签选择器求值（第 05 章）。
  • DeletionTimestamp == nil——排除正在终止的 Pod（第 36 章）。
  • buildPodFromTemplate(rs)——Spec.Template 实例化（第 12 章）。
  • 状态更新与规约更新分开——spec/status 规范（第 23 章）。

The Deployment controller is the same pattern, one level up: instead of pods, it manages ReplicaSets. The Endpoints controller is the same pattern, watching Services + Pods and writing Endpoints.

Deployment 控制器采用相同的模式，只是上移一层：它管理的是 ReplicaSet，而非 Pod。Endpoints 控制器采用相同的模式，监听 Service + Pod，并写入 Endpoints。

13.4 The Deployment Controller (Rolling Update)

13.4 Deployment 控制器（滚动更新）

This is where the toy gets fun. A Deployment rollout means:

这个玩具从这里开始变得有趣。一次 Deployment 发布意味着：

   1. New Deployment.Spec.Template hash differs from existing RS's.
   2. Create new RS with replicas=0.
   3. Scale new RS up by surge (e.g., +1) while scaling old RS down by 1.
   4. Wait until new RS's ReadyReplicas matches its replicas.
   5. Repeat 3–4 until new RS = desired and old RS = 0.
   6. Mark old RS for deletion (or keep for revision history).

   1. 新的 Deployment.Spec.Template 哈希值与现有 RS 的不同。
   2. 创建 replicas=0 的新 RS。
   3. 按 surge 扩容新 RS（例如 +1），同时将旧 RS 缩容 1。
   4. 等待新 RS 的 ReadyReplicas 与其 replicas 相匹配。
   5. 重复 3–4，直到新 RS = desired 且旧 RS = 0。
   6. 将旧 RS 标记为待删除（或保留以记录修订历史）。

Pseudocode:

伪代码：

func (c *DeploymentController) reconcile(depKey string) error {
    dep, _ := c.depLister.Get(depKey)
    if dep == nil { return nil }
    templateHash := hashTemplate(dep.Spec.Template)

    // 1. Find or create the new RS.
    rss := c.findOwnedRS(dep)
    var newRS *ReplicaSet
    var oldRSs []*ReplicaSet
    for _, rs := range rss {
        if rs.Labels["pod-template-hash"] == templateHash {
            newRS = rs
        } else {
            oldRSs = append(oldRSs, rs)
        }
    }
    if newRS == nil {
        newRS = buildRS(dep, templateHash)
        var err error
        newRS, err = c.api.CreateRS(newRS)
        if err != nil { return err }
    }

    // 2. Wait for RS observations to catch up before the next rolling step.
    for _, rs := range append(append([]*ReplicaSet(nil), oldRSs...), newRS) {
        if rs.Status.Replicas != rs.Spec.Replicas { return nil }
    }
    desired := dep.Spec.Replicas
    maxSurge := computeMaxSurge(dep)
    maxUnavailable := computeMaxUnavailable(dep)
    currentReady := countReadyAcrossAll(append(oldRSs, newRS))

    // Scale up new RS by surge.
    if newRS.Spec.Replicas < desired {
        room := desired + maxSurge - totalReplicas(append(oldRSs, newRS))
        if room > 0 {
            next := newRS.DeepCopy()
            next.Spec.Replicas += min(room, desired-newRS.Spec.Replicas)
            return c.api.UpdateRS(next) // errors are retried; cache objects stay unchanged
        }
    }

    // Scale down old RSs as new becomes ready.
    if len(oldRSs) > 0 { // maxSurge=0 may need an old replica removed first
        available := currentReady - (desired - maxUnavailable)
        if available > 0 {
            for _, old := range oldRSs {
                if old.Spec.Replicas > 0 {
                    reduce := min(available, old.Spec.Replicas)
                    next := old.DeepCopy()
                    next.Spec.Replicas -= reduce
                    return c.api.UpdateRS(next)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 3. Update Deployment status.
    next := dep.DeepCopy()
    next.Status.Replicas = observedReplicas(append(oldRSs, newRS))
    next.Status.ReadyReplicas = currentReady
    next.Status.UpdatedReplicas = newRS.Status.Replicas
    if statusEqual(dep.Status, next.Status) { return nil }
    return c.api.UpdateDeploymentStatus(next)
}

func (c *DeploymentController) reconcile(depKey string) error {
    dep, _ := c.depLister.Get(depKey)
    if dep == nil { return nil }
    templateHash := hashTemplate(dep.Spec.Template)

    // 1. 查找或创建新 RS。
    rss := c.findOwnedRS(dep)
    var newRS *ReplicaSet
    var oldRSs []*ReplicaSet
    for _, rs := range rss {
        if rs.Labels["pod-template-hash"] == templateHash {
            newRS = rs
        } else {
            oldRSs = append(oldRSs, rs)
        }
    }
    if newRS == nil {
        newRS = buildRS(dep, templateHash)
        var err error
        newRS, err = c.api.CreateRS(newRS)
        if err != nil { return err }
    }

    // 2. 在下一个滚动步骤之前，等待 RS 的观测结果跟上。
    for _, rs := range append(append([]*ReplicaSet(nil), oldRSs...), newRS) {
        if rs.Status.Replicas != rs.Spec.Replicas { return nil }
    }
    desired := dep.Spec.Replicas
    maxSurge := computeMaxSurge(dep)
    maxUnavailable := computeMaxUnavailable(dep)
    currentReady := countReadyAcrossAll(append(oldRSs, newRS))

    // 按 surge 扩容新 RS。
    if newRS.Spec.Replicas < desired {
        room := desired + maxSurge - totalReplicas(append(oldRSs, newRS))
        if room > 0 {
            next := newRS.DeepCopy()
            next.Spec.Replicas += min(room, desired-newRS.Spec.Replicas)
            return c.api.UpdateRS(next) // 发生错误时重试；缓存对象保持不变
        }
    }

    // 随着新 RS 就绪，缩容旧 RS。
    if len(oldRSs) > 0 { // maxSurge=0 可能需要先移除一个旧副本
        available := currentReady - (desired - maxUnavailable)
        if available > 0 {
            for _, old := range oldRSs {
                if old.Spec.Replicas > 0 {
                    reduce := min(available, old.Spec.Replicas)
                    next := old.DeepCopy()
                    next.Spec.Replicas -= reduce
                    return c.api.UpdateRS(next)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 3. 更新 Deployment 状态。
    next := dep.DeepCopy()
    next.Status.Replicas = observedReplicas(append(oldRSs, newRS))
    next.Status.ReadyReplicas = currentReady
    next.Status.UpdatedReplicas = newRS.Status.Replicas
    if statusEqual(dep.Status, next.Status) { return nil }
    return c.api.UpdateDeploymentStatus(next)
}
[50]

The update helpers use RV and propagate errors. Surge percentages round up, unavailable percentages round down and are capped at desired replicas; for a nonzero desired count, reject parameters that resolve to both zero. UpdatedReplicas counts observed replicas of the current template, whether Ready or not. Waiting for the next RS event after one write keeps the budget calculation tied to observed state. Unready old replicas can be reclaimed without spending the ready budget. The deployment contract includes the zero-surge and status cases checked during implementation.

更新辅助函数使用 RV 并传播错误。Surge 百分比向上取整，不可用 百分比向下取整，且以期望副本数为上限；对于非零的期望数量，拒绝 解析结果均为零的参数。UpdatedReplicas 统计观测到的当前 模板的副本数，无论是否 Ready。在一次写入之后等待下一个 RS 事件，可以使预算 计算与观测状态保持关联。未就绪的旧副本可以被回收，而无需消耗 就绪预算。Deployment 契约 包含了 实现过程中检查的零 surge 和状态用例。

[51]

This is chapter 12 §deployments distilled. The real Deployment controller has more edge cases (paused rollouts, progress deadlines, history revisions, automatic rollback) but the spine is this 30-line loop.

这是第 12 章 §deployments 的提炼。真正的 Deployment 控制器有更多边界情况（暂停发布、进度截止期限、历史修订、自动回滚），但骨架就是这段 30 行的循环。

[52]

13.5 The Endpoints Controller

13.5 Endpoints 控制器

func (c *EndpointsController) reconcile(svcKey string) error {
    ns, name := splitKey(svcKey)
    svc, err := c.svcLister.Get(svcKey)
    if err != nil && !isNotFound(err) { return err }
    if svc == nil {
        // Service deleted — retain the key even though the object is absent.
        return c.api.DeleteOwnedEndpoints(ns, name)
    }
    pods := c.podLister.ListMatching(svc.Spec.Selector, svc.Namespace)
    groups := map[EndpointPort][]EndpointAddress{}
    for _, port := range svc.Spec.Ports {
        for _, p := range pods {
            if !isReady(p) || net.ParseIP(p.Status.PodIP) == nil { continue }
            target, ok := resolveTargetPort(p, port) // omitted -> port; integer -> value; name -> Pod port
            if !ok { continue }
            backendPort := EndpointPort{Name: port.Name, Port: target, Protocol: port.Protocol}
            groups[backendPort] = append(groups[backendPort], EndpointAddress{
                IP: p.Status.PodIP, NodeName: &p.Spec.NodeName,
            })
        }
    }
    subsets := sortedEndpointSubsets(groups)
    eps := &Endpoints{
        Metadata: ObjectMeta{Namespace: svc.Namespace, Name: svc.Name},
        Subsets:  subsets,
    }
    return c.api.UpsertEndpoints(eps)
}

func (c *EndpointsController) reconcile(svcKey string) error {
    ns, name := splitKey(svcKey)
    svc, err := c.svcLister.Get(svcKey)
    if err != nil && !isNotFound(err) { return err }
    if svc == nil {
        // Service 已删除——即使对象不存在，也保留键。
        return c.api.DeleteOwnedEndpoints(ns, name)
    }
    pods := c.podLister.ListMatching(svc.Spec.Selector, svc.Namespace)
    groups := map[EndpointPort][]EndpointAddress{}
    for _, port := range svc.Spec.Ports {
        for _, p := range pods {
            if !isReady(p) || net.ParseIP(p.Status.PodIP) == nil { continue }
            target, ok := resolveTargetPort(p, port) // 省略 -> port；整数 -> 该值；名称 -> Pod 端口
            if !ok { continue }
            backendPort := EndpointPort{Name: port.Name, Port: target, Protocol: port.Protocol}
            groups[backendPort] = append(groups[backendPort], EndpointAddress{
                IP: p.Status.PodIP, NodeName: &p.Spec.NodeName,
            })
        }
    }
    subsets := sortedEndpointSubsets(groups)
    eps := &Endpoints{
        Metadata: ObjectMeta{Namespace: svc.Namespace, Name: svc.Name},
        Subsets:  subsets,
    }
    return c.api.UpsertEndpoints(eps)
}
[53]

For each Pod resolve an omitted targetPort to the Service port, an integer to that port, or a name to the matching container port/protocol. Group addresses by resolved port; include only Ready applications with valid Pod IPs, sort output, and skip unchanged writes. Pod old/new label and Ready changes enqueue the affected Service keys. Preserve selectorless/manual Endpoints; automatic Endpoints carry the Service UID so same-name replacements can be distinguished.

对于每个 Pod，将省略的 targetPort 解析为 Service 端口，将整数解析为该端口，或将 名称解析为匹配的容器端口/协议。按解析出的端口对地址分组；只包含具有有效 Pod IP 的 Ready 应用，对输出排序，并跳过未变化的写入。Pod 的新旧标签和 Ready 变化会使受影响的 Service 键入队。保留无选择器/手动 Endpoints；自动 Endpoints 携带 Service UID，以便区分同名替换对象。

[54]

That is the shape of the Endpoints controller. Real K8s also has the EndpointSlice controller (chapter 14 §endpointslice) for scalability beyond ~1000 endpoints — same loop, slightly different output shape.

这就是 Endpoints 控制器的样子。真正的 K8s 还有 EndpointSlice 控制器（第 14 章 §endpointslice），用于扩展到大约 1000 个以上的端点——循环相同，输出形态略有不同。

[55]

13.6 The GC Controller (Optional in minik8s)

13.6 GC 控制器（在 minik8s 中可选）

If we want OwnerReferences-driven cascade deletes (chapter 36), we add a simple GC controller:

如果我们想要由 OwnerReferences 驱动的级联删除（第 36 章），就添加一个简单的 GC 控制器：

func (gc *GCController) Run() {
    // Watch every type. Build owner graph: child UID → list of owner refs.
    // On parent delete, find children, schedule deletion (respecting policy).
}

func (gc *GCController) Run() {
    // 监听每种类型。构建拥有者图：子对象 UID → 拥有者引用列表。
    // 父对象删除时，查找子对象，安排删除（遵循策略）。
}

For the toy demo we hard-code the cascade in the parent controllers (Deployment deletes its RSs; RS deletes its Pods). The real GC controller is generic over all owner-ref graphs and handles cycles, finalizers, and the foreground/background/orphan policies (chapter 36). Adding it later is mechanical.

对于这个玩具演示，我们在父控制器中硬编码级联操作（Deployment 删除其 RS；RS 删除其 Pod）。真正的 GC 控制器通用于所有拥有者引用图，并处理环、finalizer 以及前台/后台/孤儿策略（第 36 章）。以后添加它是机械性的工作。

14. Phase 9 — mini-cni: One Hundred Lines of Bridge

14. 阶段 9 — mini-cni：一百行网桥

The Container Network Interface contract is delightfully simple: the kubelet exec's a binary, passes JSON on stdin (and env vars), gets JSON on stdout. The binary's job is to "do whatever networking is needed" inside the netns it was told to operate on.

容器网络接口的契约简单得令人愉快：kubelet 执行一个二进制程序，通过 stdin（以及环境变量）传入 JSON，从 stdout 获取 JSON。这个二进制程序的工作是在被告知要操作的 netns 内“完成所需的一切网络配置”。

14.1 The Contract

14.1 契约

   CNI plugin invocation:
     argv: /opt/cni/bin/bridge
     env:  CNI_COMMAND=ADD            (or DEL, CHECK, VERSION)
           CNI_CONTAINERID=<podID>
           CNI_NETNS=/var/run/netns/<podID>
           CNI_IFNAME=eth0
           CNI_PATH=/opt/cni/bin
           CNI_ARGS=K8S_POD_NAMESPACE=default;K8S_POD_NAME=foo;…
     stdin: network config JSON
     stdout: result JSON

   CNI 插件调用：
     argv: /opt/cni/bin/bridge
     env:  CNI_COMMAND=ADD            （或 DEL、CHECK、VERSION）
           CNI_CONTAINERID=<podID>
           CNI_NETNS=/var/run/netns/<podID>
           CNI_IFNAME=eth0
           CNI_PATH=/opt/cni/bin
           CNI_ARGS=K8S_POD_NAMESPACE=default;K8S_POD_NAME=foo;…
     stdin: 网络配置 JSON
     stdout: 结果 JSON

Per chapter 15 §cni-spec, the result for an ADD contains the assigned IP, gateway, DNS, etc.

根据第 15 章 §cni-spec，ADD 的结果包含分配的 IP、网关、DNS 等。

14.2 The 100-Line Plugin

14.2 100 行的插件

// File: minik8s/cni/bridge/main.go
package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "fmt"
    "net"
    "os"
    "crypto/sha256"
    "errors"
    "syscall"
    "github.com/vishvananda/netlink"
    "github.com/vishvananda/netns"
)

type NetConf struct {
    CNIVersion string `json:"cniVersion"`
    Name       string `json:"name"`
    Type       string `json:"type"`
    Bridge     string `json:"bridge"`
    IPAM       struct {
        Type   string `json:"type"`
        Subnet string `json:"subnet"`
        Range  string `json:"rangeStart-rangeEnd"`
        StateFile string `json:"stateFile"` // we hardcode: file-backed pool
    } `json:"ipam"`
}

type Result struct {
    CNIVersion string `json:"cniVersion"`
    IPs []IPConfig `json:"ips"`
}
type IPConfig struct {
    Version string `json:"version"`
    Address string `json:"address"`
    Gateway string `json:"gateway"`
}

func main() {
    cmd := os.Getenv("CNI_COMMAND")
    var cfg NetConf
    json.NewDecoder(os.Stdin).Decode(&cfg)
    switch cmd {
    case "ADD":  cmdAdd(&cfg)
    case "DEL":  cmdDel(&cfg)
    case "CHECK": must(checkAllocatedInterfaceAddressRoute(&cfg))
